MAIS Manim v2 is substantially complete as a pure TypeScript source-architecture/runtime package, the focused A11 MAIS Manim browser gate is green, the A22 pruned-staging build path is green, and the A18/A06 automated teaching-quality precheck is green for every registered Manim family. The A06 review-package split is executable as a deterministic package matrix plus a package-level handoff checklist matrix, and the 8 coarse packages now expand into 20 bounded review slices capped at 24 files each. The 12 concrete representative scenes now have A06-confirmed teaching-review cases, a grouped A18 human-review brief, a typed signoff matrix that keeps final acceptance pending A18 review, source-backed rendered-scene inspection targets with stable scene IDs/family IDs/selectors, a concrete browser route packet for A18 rendered review, a per-scene review dossier with nine proof points per case, a final rendered teaching review packet joining all 12 browser routes to all 108 proof points, a final decision ledger expanding the remaining A18 work into 60 pending criterion decisions, a final-decision intake reducer that can apply future A18 approve/revision/block records without inventing signoff, and an A06 source-confirmation ledger that proves all 60 decisions have source evidence while still leaving A18 final acceptance pending. A06 now also has release-readiness evidence, an A22 clean-slice release manifest for the Manim source package, a Manim v2 goal gate, a cross-agent completion handoff that maps the four requested workstreams, a completion closure queue that turns the remaining open work into owner-action rows, an acceptance checklist that defines the exact owner evidence required for each row, an evidence intake reducer that can validate submitted A11/A22/A18 owner evidence without bypassing gates, a completion status summary that separates A06 source-architecture readiness from cross-agent goal completion, an owner-evidence request packet that groups missing A11/A18/A22 evidence into submit-ready records, an owner-gate rerun intake that validates future A11/A18/A22 gate rerun records after evidence is covered, an owner-gate rerun command packet that gives A11/A22/A18 executable next-step commands or manual review routes, an owner-gate transcript request packet that turns those command/manual-review rows into pending transcript templates without accepting evidence, an owner-gate rerun command transcript intake that validates command/manual-review transcripts against those rows, an owner-gate rerun command-evidence intake that requires every command/manual-review row before synthesizing owner gate rerun records, a verified closure pipeline that composes command-row evidence, owner-gate rerun intake, and final objective audit, a transcript verified-closure pipeline that starts from raw command/manual-review transcripts before feeding that verified closure path, a final closure audit that consumes rerun intake before allowing completion, an objective completion audit that verifies the four requested workstreams without redefining completion, a final objective-audit request packet that asks for the final `4/4` audit record without accepting it, a final objective-audit record intake that validates accepted/blocked/invalid submitted `4/4` final audit records without marking the thread goal complete, a final objective verified-closure pipeline that requires transcripts, command evidence, final-audit request readiness, record-intake acceptance, and verified closure before the synthetic complete path can pass, a final objective proof ledger that expands the four proof evidence IDs into auditable requirement rows, and a final owner closure packet that pairs each A11/A18/A22 transcript request with the required final proof evidence IDs. A06 review packaging is ready, while A11 browser regression, A22 clean release, and A18/A06 final teaching signoff still require owner action. The cross-agent handoff now consumes the review-slice matrix, the A22 release-slice manifest, the A18 rendered-review route packet, and the final decision ledger, exposing `reviewSlices=20@24`, `releaseSlice=387/0-forbidden`, `a18Routes=12/12`, and `a18Decisions=60/60-pending`; the closure queue expands the 3 open workstreams into 11 owner actions with `0` A06-only closeable actions, the acceptance checklist keeps all 11 rows `pending-owner-evidence`, the evidence intake currently records `0/22` accepted required evidence IDs, the owner-evidence request packet groups those missing records into A11 `6`, A18 `10`, and A22 `6` required evidence IDs, the command packet now emits A11 browser rerun commands, A22 release/preflight/staging commands, and 12 A18 route-review rows, the transcript request packet exposes those rows as pending templates with exact required fields, the command-transcript intake validates transcript command text, exit codes, route hrefs, selectors, and A18 decisions before producing command evidence records, the command-evidence intake requires all owner rows before producing A11/A18/A22 gate records, the transcript verified-closure pipeline keeps raw transcripts as the first completion gate before the verified closure pipeline waits for final objective audit, the final objective-audit request packet asks for accepted `4/4` audit evidence after owner gates are covered, the final objective-audit record intake validates that evidence shape before final closure consumes it, the final objective verified-closure pipeline joins those last gates into one auditable reducer, the final objective proof ledger reports `0/4` final-record proof IDs covered in the current no-record state with `1` source-ready proof and `3` pending owner proofs, the final owner closure packet reports `owners=A11,A18,A22`, `transcripts=3` in its focused fixture, `proofs=0/4`, and `a06SourceReady=1`, the completion status summary reports `source-architecture-ready-goal-blocked` with remaining owner agents `A11`, `A18`, and `A22`, and the objective audit reports `1/4` requirements proven with A11/A18/A22 still remaining. Completion evidence now distinguishes source/package readiness, clean-slice readiness, route/proof readiness, owner-action closure work, owner-evidence acceptance criteria, owner-evidence intake coverage, per-owner evidence request packets, owner-gate rerun command coverage, owner transcript request templates, command validation, command-row evidence coverage, final owner closure submissions, transcript-to-verified-closure coverage, verified closure pipeline coverage, owner-gate rerun intake coverage, final closure audit coverage, final objective-audit request readiness, final objective-audit record intake readiness, final objective verified-closure readiness, final proof-ledger readiness, source-vs-goal status, objective-level proof, and still-pending A18 final acceptance. It is still not release-complete until A18 completes final curriculum-fit/instructional signoff, A11/A22 close the remaining broader Visualization Lab regression/release evidence, and A22 clears the current disk-space preflight blocker.

Latest A06/A18 final-decision intake non-canonical-status update: the A18 teaching final-decision intake now reports padded but otherwise valid submitted criterion-record statuses separately before closing final signoff. Padded statuses such as ` a18-approved ` report `status=blocked-non-canonical-a18-final-decision-record-statuses`, expose `nonCanonicalRecordStatuses`, stay out of `unsupportedRecordStatuses`, serialize stable non-canonical-status data attributes, and keep `canMarkA18GateComplete=false`.

Latest A06/A18 final-decision intake missing-status update: the A18 teaching final-decision intake now reports empty or whitespace-only submitted criterion-record statuses separately before closing final signoff. Blank statuses report `status=blocked-missing-a18-final-decision-record-statuses`, expose `missingRecordStatusCount`, stay out of `unsupportedRecordStatuses`, serialize a stable missing-status data attribute, and keep `canMarkA18GateComplete=false`.

Latest A06/A18 final-decision intake unsupported-status update: the A18 teaching final-decision intake now blocks unsupported submitted criterion-record statuses before closing final signoff. Runtime statuses such as `approved` report `status=blocked-unsupported-a18-final-decision-record-statuses`, expose `unsupportedRecordStatuses`, serialize stable unsupported-status data attributes, and keep `canMarkA18GateComplete=false` instead of serializing a misleading `a18-final-approved` status.

Latest A06/A18 final-decision intake duplicate-record update: the A18 teaching final-decision intake now blocks duplicate valid A18 criterion records before closing final signoff. Duplicate `caseId:criterion` submissions report `status=blocked-duplicate-a18-final-decision-records`, expose `duplicateRecordDecisionKeys`, serialize a stable duplicate-record data attribute, and keep `canMarkA18GateComplete=false` even when all 60 decisions otherwise look approved.

Latest A06 final completion dossier update: a pure TypeScript final completion dossier now summarizes the last closure path into six owner-review stages: command transcripts, command evidence, final objective-audit request, final objective-audit record, final objective proof ledger, and verified closure. It consumes the pipeline proof-ledger fields directly, exposes `proofs=0/4`, `sourceReady=1`, `pending=3`, and keeps the current source state at `387` Manim TS/TSX files, `20` review slices, evidence package `86` files (`43` production/runtime and `43` tests), `0` missing, `0` duplicate, and `0` unclassified. Its current status remains `pending-command-transcripts` / `source-architecture-ready-goal-blocked`, with `canMarkThreadGoalComplete=false`, because real A11/A22 command transcripts, A18 route-review transcripts, owner rerun records, and the final `4/4` objective audit are still pending.

Latest A06 final completion dossier proof-ledger block propagation update: the dossier now treats `blocked-duplicate-final-objective-proof-ids` from the final proof ledger as `blocked-final-objective-proof-ledger` instead of allowing a synthetic pipeline `complete` status to display as complete. The proof-ledger stage becomes blocked, the dossier remains `canMarkThreadGoalComplete=false`, and stable dossier data attributes surface the blocked status for A11/A18/A22 owner review.

Latest A06 final objective verified-closure proof-ledger block propagation update: the final objective verified-closure pipeline now treats `blocked-duplicate-final-objective-proof-ids` from the final proof ledger as `blocked-final-objective-proof-ledger` before considering the synthetic verified-closure `complete` path. The proof ledger also leaves duplicate-proof rows un-covered even when a malformed final audit record repeats the same proof ID, so pipeline data attributes report blocked status, `proofs=0/4`, `pending=3`, and `canMarkThreadGoalComplete=false`.

Latest A06 final objective verified-closure summary truth update: the final objective verified-closure pipeline summary now derives `canComplete` from the pipeline-level `canMarkThreadGoalComplete` decision instead of the inner verified-closure reducer. This keeps blocked proof-ledger states from serializing `verified=complete:canComplete=true` when the pipeline status is `blocked-final-objective-proof-ledger`; stable summary attributes now report `canComplete=false` alongside the blocked proof-ledger status.

Latest A06 owner-gate rerun duplicate-evidence update: the owner-gate rerun intake now blocks duplicate A11/A18/A22 owner-gate rerun evidence IDs before final audit readiness. Duplicate owner-gate rerun records report `blocked-owner-gate-rerun`, expose `duplicateRecordEvidenceIds`, count the duplicated records as invalid, serialize a stable duplicate-record data attribute, and keep `readyForFinalAudit=false`.

Latest A06 owner-gate rerun duplicate-step update: the owner-gate rerun intake now blocks duplicate A11/A18/A22 owner-gate rerun step submissions before final audit readiness, even when the duplicated step uses a distinct evidence ID. Duplicate owner-gate rerun records now expose `duplicateRecordStepIds`, count the duplicated step records as invalid, serialize a stable duplicate-step data attribute, and keep `readyForFinalAudit=false`.

Latest A06 final objective unknown-proof update: the final objective audit record intake already rejected final `4/4` records that included proof IDs outside the requested four requirement proofs; it now also exposes `unknownRequirementProofEvidenceIds`, serializes a stable unknown-proof data attribute, and records `unknownProofIds=...` in the summary so A11/A18/A22 owner evidence mistakes are diagnosable instead of opaque invalid records.

Latest A06 final objective missing-proof update: the same final objective audit record intake now exposes `missingRequirementProofEvidenceIds`, serializes a stable missing-proof data attribute, and records `missingProofIds=...` in the summary when a submitted final `4/4` record omits one or more requested proof IDs. This keeps owner corrections precise for A11 browser proof, A18/A06 teaching proof, and A22 clean release proof gaps.

Latest A06 final objective duplicate-submitted-proof update: the final objective audit record intake now also exposes `duplicateRequirementProofEvidenceIds`, serializes a stable duplicate-proof data attribute, and records `duplicateProofIds=...` in the summary when a submitted final `4/4` record repeats a proof ID. The record remains invalid, but A11/A18/A22 owner correction now gets an exact duplicate proof ID instead of an opaque invalid final audit record.

Latest A06 final objective missing-proof duplicate-diagnostic update: the final objective audit record intake now keeps empty or whitespace-only submitted `requirementProofEvidenceIds` out of `duplicateRequirementProofEvidenceIds`. Two blank submitted proof IDs remain blocked through missing, unknown, and non-canonical proof diagnostics, serialize `duplicateProofIds=none`, and keep final closure pending until real canonical per-requirement proof IDs are supplied.

Latest A06 final objective duplicate-submitted-owner-gate update: the final objective audit record intake now also exposes `duplicateOwnerGateRerunEvidenceIds`, serializes a stable duplicate-owner-gate data attribute, and records `duplicateOwnerGateIds=...` in the summary when a submitted final `4/4` record repeats an owner-gate rerun evidence ID. The record remains invalid, but A11/A18/A22 owner correction now gets an exact duplicate owner-gate ID instead of an opaque invalid final audit record.

Latest A06 final objective unknown-owner-gate update: the final objective audit record intake now also exposes `unknownOwnerGateRerunEvidenceIds`, serializes a stable unknown-owner-gate data attribute, and records `unknownOwnerGateIds=...` in the summary when a submitted final `4/4` record includes owner-gate rerun evidence IDs outside the request packet. The record remains invalid, but A11/A18/A22 owner correction now gets the exact foreign owner-gate ID instead of an opaque invalid final audit record.

Latest A06 final objective missing-owner-gate update: the final objective audit record intake now also exposes `missingOwnerGateRerunEvidenceIds`, serializes a stable missing-owner-gate data attribute, and records `missingOwnerGateIds=...` in the summary when a submitted final `4/4` record omits one or more owner-gate rerun evidence IDs requested by the final-audit packet. The record remains invalid, but A11/A18/A22 owner correction now gets the exact omitted owner-gate ID instead of an opaque invalid final audit record.

Latest A06 final objective duplicate-accepted-record update: the final objective audit record intake now blocks ambiguous closure evidence when more than one otherwise valid accepted final `4/4` audit record is submitted. It exposes `duplicateAcceptedFinalAuditRecordIds`, serializes a stable duplicate-accepted-record data attribute, and records `duplicateAcceptedRecordIds=...` in the summary so A11/A18/A22 owner correction gets the exact competing final record IDs instead of silently choosing the first sorted record.

Latest A06 final objective duplicate-submitted-record update: the final objective audit record intake now also blocks duplicate submitted final audit record evidence IDs across accepted, blocked, or invalid records. It exposes `duplicateSubmittedFinalAuditRecordEvidenceIds`, serializes a stable duplicate-submitted-record data attribute, and records `duplicateSubmittedRecordIds=...` in the summary so A11/A18/A22 owner correction gets the exact reused final record ID instead of a vague blocked final audit state.

Latest A06 final objective missing-submitted-record-evidence update: the final objective audit record intake now also counts submitted final audit records with an empty final record `evidenceId`. It exposes `missingSubmittedFinalAuditRecordEvidenceIdCount`, serializes a stable missing-submitted-record-evidence count attribute, and records `missingSubmittedRecordEvidenceCount=...` in the summary so A11/A18/A22 owner correction can distinguish a malformed blank final record ID from other invalid final-audit fields.

Latest A06 final objective whitespace-submitted-record-evidence update: the final objective audit record intake now treats whitespace-only final record `evidenceId` values as missing submitted record evidence. The accepted-record shape, duplicate submitted record filtering, and missing-submitted-record-evidence counter all use the trimmed evidence ID predicate, so a whitespace-only final `4/4` record can no longer become accepted closure evidence.

Latest A06 final objective non-canonical-submitted-record update: the final objective audit record intake now also blocks non-empty submitted final record `evidenceId` values that would change when trimmed. It exposes `nonCanonicalSubmittedFinalAuditRecordEvidenceIds`, serializes a stable non-canonical-submitted-record data attribute, and records `nonCanonicalSubmittedRecordIds=...` in the summary so A11/A18/A22 owner correction gets the exact padded final record ID instead of treating it as accepted final closure evidence.

Latest A06 final objective mismatched-target update: the final objective audit record intake now exposes submitted final audit record `target` values that do not match `mathSceneV2ObjectiveCompletionAudit`. It serializes `mismatchedFinalAuditRecordTargets`, adds a stable mismatched-target data attribute, and records `mismatchedTargets=...` in the summary so malformed owner final-audit records can be corrected by target value instead of appearing as generic invalid evidence.

Latest A06 final objective non-canonical-target update: the final objective audit record intake now also exposes submitted final audit record `target` values that would change when trimmed. It serializes `nonCanonicalFinalAuditRecordTargets`, adds a stable non-canonical-target data attribute, and records `nonCanonicalTargets=...` in the summary so padded owner final-audit target strings can be corrected directly instead of appearing only as generic mismatched target evidence.

Latest A06 final objective unsupported-status update: the final objective audit record intake now exposes submitted final audit record `status` values outside the supported accepted/blocked final-audit vocabulary. It serializes `unsupportedFinalAuditRecordStatuses`, adds a stable unsupported-status data attribute, and records `unsupportedStatuses=...` in the summary so owner final-audit status typos can be corrected directly instead of appearing only as generic invalid final-audit records.

Latest A06 final objective non-canonical-status update: the final objective audit record intake now also exposes submitted final audit record `status` values that would change when trimmed. It serializes `nonCanonicalFinalAuditRecordStatuses`, adds a stable non-canonical-status data attribute, and records `nonCanonicalStatuses=...` in the summary so padded owner final-audit status strings can be corrected directly instead of appearing only as unsupported final-audit statuses.

Latest A06 final closure non-canonical-evidence-id update: the downstream final closure audit now rejects final objective audit records whose non-empty `evidenceId` would change when trimmed. A padded final audit evidence ID now produces `blocked-final-objective-audit` with `finalAuditRecordStatus=invalid-final-objective-audit` instead of completing the thread goal, so a direct final closure reducer call cannot bypass the canonical final-record ID guard already enforced by the record intake.

Latest A06 final closure non-canonical-proof-id update: the downstream final closure audit now rejects final objective audit records whose submitted requirement proof evidence IDs are blank after trimming or would change when trimmed. A padded proof ID now produces `blocked-final-objective-audit` with `finalAuditRecordStatus=invalid-final-objective-audit`, so a direct final closure reducer call cannot bypass the canonical proof-ID guard already enforced by the final objective record intake.

Latest A06 final closure unknown-proof-id update: the downstream final closure audit now derives the required final objective proof evidence IDs from `objectiveAudit.requirements` and requires the submitted final audit record to match that set. An arbitrary canonical proof ID now produces `blocked-final-objective-audit` with `finalAuditRecordStatus=invalid-final-objective-audit`, so a direct final closure reducer call cannot replace A06/A11/A18/A22 objective proof IDs with unrelated strings.

Latest A06 final closure unsupported-status update: the downstream final closure audit now runtime-validates submitted final audit record `status` values before considering proof counts. An unsupported status such as `passed` now produces `blocked-final-objective-audit` with `finalAuditRecordStatus=invalid-final-objective-audit`, so a direct final closure reducer call cannot complete the thread goal with a malformed status value after owner gates pass.

Latest A06 owner-gate rerun non-canonical-evidence update: the owner-gate rerun intake now exposes accepted or blocked A11/A18/A22 rerun records whose non-empty submitted `evidenceId` would change when trimmed as `nonCanonicalRecordEvidenceIds`. A padded owner-gate evidence ID now remains `blocked-owner-gate-rerun`, serializes `data-viz-manim-v2-owner-gate-rerun-intake-non-canonical-record-ids`, records `nonCanonicalRecordIds=...` in the summary, keeps the affected owner row pending, and prevents final audit readiness until the owner rerun evidence ID is canonical.

Latest A06/A11/A18/A22 owner-gate rerun non-canonical-evidence-row update: the owner-gate rerun intake now exposes padded submitted owner rerun `evidenceId` values as stable owner/step row diagnostics before final audit readiness can be trusted. The reducer reports `nonCanonicalRecordEvidenceIdRows`, emits `data-viz-manim-v2-owner-gate-rerun-intake-non-canonical-record-id-rows`, preserves `nonCanonicalRecordEvidenceIds`, and keeps final audit readiness blocked until the owner submits a canonical evidence ID for the exact owner-gate rerun step. Checks: focused owner-gate rerun intake `24/24` after intended RED (`23/24`, row field contract absent), adjacent owner transcript/command-evidence/owner-rerun/verified-closure suite `146/146`, all `mathSceneV2*.test.ts` `223/223`, A06/A11/A18/A22-adjacent evidence suite `296/296`, and `npm run type-check` passed.

Latest A06 owner-gate rerun non-canonical-evidence duplicate-diagnostic update: the owner-gate rerun intake now keeps padded submitted owner rerun `evidenceId` values out of `duplicateRecordEvidenceIds`. Two identical padded A11/A18 rerun evidence IDs remain blocked through `nonCanonicalRecordEvidenceIds`, serialize `duplicateRecordIds=none` plus `nonCanonicalRecordIds=...`, keep the affected owner rows pending, and prevent final audit readiness until canonical owner rerun evidence IDs are supplied.

Latest A06 owner-gate rerun missing-evidence-id update: the owner-gate rerun intake now exposes accepted or blocked A11/A18/A22 rerun records whose submitted `evidenceId` is empty or whitespace-only as `missingRecordEvidenceIdCount`. A blank owner-gate evidence ID remains `blocked-owner-gate-rerun`, serializes `data-viz-manim-v2-owner-gate-rerun-intake-missing-record-id-count`, records `missingRecordIds=...` in the summary, keeps the affected owner row pending, and prevents final audit readiness until the owner rerun evidence ID is corrected.

Latest A06 owner-gate rerun omitted-evidence-id update: the owner-gate rerun intake now treats accepted or blocked A11/A18/A22 rerun records whose submitted `evidenceId` property is omitted as missing evidence instead of throwing during duplicate or matching checks. An omitted owner-gate evidence ID remains `blocked-owner-gate-rerun`, increments `missingRecordEvidenceIdCount`, serializes `data-viz-manim-v2-owner-gate-rerun-intake-missing-record-id-count`, records `missingRecordIds=...` in the summary, keeps the affected owner row pending, and prevents final audit readiness until the owner rerun evidence ID is supplied.

Latest A06 owner-gate rerun missing-evidence duplicate-diagnostic update: the owner-gate rerun intake now keeps empty or whitespace-only submitted owner rerun `evidenceId` values out of `duplicateRecordEvidenceIds`. Two blank A11/A18 rerun evidence IDs remain blocked through `missingRecordEvidenceIdCount`, serialize `duplicateRecordIds=none` plus `missingRecordIds=2`, keep the affected owner rows pending, and prevent final audit readiness until real owner rerun evidence IDs are supplied.

Latest A06 owner-gate rerun missing-step-id update: the owner-gate rerun intake now exposes accepted or blocked A11/A18/A22 rerun records whose submitted `stepId` is empty or whitespace-only as `missingRecordStepIdCount`. A blank owner-gate step ID remains `blocked-owner-gate-rerun`, serializes `data-viz-manim-v2-owner-gate-rerun-intake-missing-step-id-count`, records `missingStepIds=...` in the summary, keeps the affected owner row pending, and prevents final audit readiness until the owner rerun step ID is corrected.

Latest A06 owner-gate rerun missing-step duplicate-diagnostic update: the owner-gate rerun intake now keeps empty or whitespace-only submitted owner rerun `stepId` values out of `duplicateRecordStepIds`. Two A11 rerun submissions with distinct evidence IDs and the same blank step ID remain blocked through `missingRecordStepIdCount`, serialize `duplicateRecordSteps=none` plus `missingStepIds=2`, keep the affected owner row pending, and prevent final audit readiness until real owner rerun step IDs are supplied.

Latest A06 owner-gate rerun non-canonical-step-id update: the owner-gate rerun intake now exposes accepted or blocked A11/A18/A22 rerun records whose non-empty submitted `stepId` would change when trimmed as `nonCanonicalRecordStepIds`. A padded owner-gate step ID remains `blocked-owner-gate-rerun`, serializes `data-viz-manim-v2-owner-gate-rerun-intake-non-canonical-step-ids`, records `nonCanonicalStepIds=...` in the summary, keeps the affected owner row pending, and prevents final audit readiness until the owner rerun step ID is canonical.

Latest A06 owner-gate rerun unknown-step-id update: the owner-gate rerun intake now exposes accepted or blocked A11/A18/A22 rerun records whose canonical submitted `stepId` is not one of the requested owner-gate rerun steps as `unknownRecordStepIds`. An unknown owner-gate step ID remains `blocked-owner-gate-rerun`, serializes `data-viz-manim-v2-owner-gate-rerun-intake-unknown-step-ids`, records `unknownStepIds=...` in the summary, stays out of the mismatched-owner bucket, keeps the affected owner row pending, and prevents final audit readiness until the owner rerun step ID matches the requested gate.

Latest A06 owner-gate rerun missing-target update: the owner-gate rerun intake now exposes accepted or blocked A11/A18/A22 rerun records whose submitted `rerunTarget` is empty or whitespace-only as `missingRecordRerunTargetCount`. A blank owner-gate target remains `blocked-owner-gate-rerun`, serializes `data-viz-manim-v2-owner-gate-rerun-intake-missing-target-count`, records `missingTargets=...` in the summary, stays out of the mismatched-target bucket, keeps the affected owner row pending, and prevents final audit readiness until the owner rerun target is corrected.

Latest A06 owner-gate rerun non-canonical-target update: the owner-gate rerun intake now exposes accepted or blocked A11/A18/A22 rerun records whose non-empty submitted `rerunTarget` would change when trimmed as `nonCanonicalRecordRerunTargets`. A padded owner-gate target remains `blocked-owner-gate-rerun`, serializes `data-viz-manim-v2-owner-gate-rerun-intake-non-canonical-targets`, records `nonCanonicalTargets=...` in the summary, stays out of the mismatched-target bucket, keeps the affected owner row pending, and prevents final audit readiness until the owner rerun target is canonical.

Latest A06 final objective mismatched-requirement-count update: the final objective audit record intake now exposes submitted final audit record `requirementCount` values that do not match the requested final objective requirement count. It serializes `mismatchedFinalAuditRecordRequirementCounts`, adds a stable mismatched-requirement-count data attribute, and records `mismatchedRequirementCounts=...` in the summary so owner final-audit corrections can identify a wrong submitted/required count pair instead of seeing only generic invalid evidence.

Latest A06 final objective mismatched-proven-count update: the final objective audit record intake now exposes accepted final audit records whose `provenRequirementCount` does not match the requested final objective requirement count. It serializes `mismatchedFinalAuditRecordProvenRequirementCounts`, adds a stable mismatched-proven-requirement-count data attribute, and records `mismatchedProvenRequirementCounts=...` in the summary so an accepted-looking `3/4` final record cannot hide behind generic invalid evidence.

Latest A06 final objective non-canonical-proof-id update: the final objective audit record intake now exposes submitted requirement proof evidence IDs that would change when trimmed. It serializes `nonCanonicalRequirementProofEvidenceIds`, adds a stable non-canonical-proof data attribute, and records `nonCanonicalProofIds=...` in the summary so padded A06/A11/A18/A22 proof IDs can be corrected directly instead of appearing only as paired unknown/missing proof evidence.

Latest A06 final objective non-canonical-owner-gate update: the final objective audit record intake now exposes submitted owner-gate rerun evidence IDs that would change when trimmed. It serializes `nonCanonicalOwnerGateRerunEvidenceIds`, adds a stable non-canonical-owner-gate data attribute, and records `nonCanonicalOwnerGateIds=...` in the summary so padded A11/A18/A22 owner-gate IDs can be corrected directly instead of appearing only as paired unknown/missing owner-gate evidence.

Latest A06 final objective proof-ID hardening update: the final objective-audit request packet now requires `requirementProofEvidenceIds`, and the final objective-audit record intake rejects a bare `4/4` record that only lists owner-gate evidence IDs. A valid final record must carry one proof evidence ID for each of the four objective requirements: A06 review-package/source architecture, A11 browser visual/interaction regression, A18/A06 teaching-quality confirmation, and A22 clean release gate. This keeps route-level or owner-level evidence from being summarized as an unaudited `4/4`.

Latest A06 final objective proof ledger update: a pure TypeScript ledger now expands the four required `requirementProofEvidenceIds` into per-requirement rows. In the current no-final-record state it reports `status=pending-final-objective-proof-record`, `proofs=0/4`, `sourceReady=1`, `pending=3`, remaining owners `A11,A18,A22`, and `canMarkThreadGoalComplete=false`; in the synthetic accepted-record path it marks all four rows `final-record-proof-covered` without bypassing final closure. The ledger now also blocks duplicate required proof IDs with `blocked-duplicate-final-objective-proof-ids`, exposes `duplicateRequiredProofEvidenceIds`, and serializes a stable duplicate-proof attribute so one proof ID cannot cover multiple final objective requirements.

Latest A06 final objective proof ledger blank-required-proof duplicate-diagnostic update: the proof ledger now keeps empty or whitespace-only requested `requiredProofEvidenceId` rows out of `duplicateRequiredProofEvidenceIds`. Blank request rows remain pending and uncovered instead of blocking as duplicate final proof IDs, so duplicate-proof diagnostics stay reserved for canonical per-requirement proof IDs.

Latest A06 final owner closure packet update: a pure TypeScript packet now pairs A11/A18/A22 transcript request rows with their final objective proof evidence IDs. In the current request state it reports `status=pending-owner-closure-submissions`, owners `A11,A18,A22`, pending owner proofs `3`, A06 source-ready proofs `1`, and keeps `canMarkThreadGoalComplete=false`; blocked transcript requests keep owner closure blocked, duplicate owner transcript packets now report `blocked-duplicate-owner-transcript-requests` with `duplicateTranscriptOwnerAgentIds`, duplicate transcript row evidence IDs now report `blocked-duplicate-owner-transcript-rows` with `duplicateTranscriptRowEvidenceIds`, transcript request rows whose outer evidence ID does not match `request-${ownerAgentId}-${rowEvidenceId}-transcript` now report `blocked-owner-transcript-row-identity-mismatch` with `mismatchedTranscriptRequestRowEvidenceIds`, transcript rows whose required fields drift from command or route-review transcript contracts now report `blocked-owner-transcript-required-fields-mismatch` with `mismatchedTranscriptRequiredFieldRowEvidenceIds`, transcript templates whose owner, row evidence ID, pending evidence ID, kind, command, href, or selector do not match their source row now report `blocked-owner-transcript-template-mismatch` with `mismatchedTranscriptTemplateRowEvidenceIds`, stale transcript summary totals now report `blocked-owner-transcript-count-mismatch` with `transcriptCountMismatchReasons`, proof-required owners represented by empty transcript packets now report `blocked-empty-owner-transcript-requests` with `emptyTranscriptOwnerAgentIds`, owner transcript packets with rows assigned to a different owner now report `blocked-owner-transcript-row-mismatch` with `mismatchedTranscriptRowOwnerAgentIds`, owner transcript packets whose pending/command/route counts do not match their rows now report `blocked-owner-transcript-row-count-mismatch` with `transcriptRowCountMismatchOwnerAgentIds`, owner rows with no matching final proof ID now report `blocked-owner-proof-requests` with `missingProofOwnerAgentIds`, and proof-ledger owners with no matching transcript request now report `blocked-owner-transcript-proof-mismatch` with `missingTranscriptOwnerAgentIds` instead of making required owner proof work invisible.

Latest A06 final owner closure acceptance-policy update: the same pure TypeScript packet now recomputes transcript acceptance policy by row kind before owner closure. Command rows must expose `expectedExitCode=0` and no review decisions, while A18 teaching-route rows must expose no command exit code and exactly `approved`, `revision-required`, and `blocked` as allowed review decisions. Drift now reports `blocked-owner-transcript-acceptance-policy-mismatch` with `mismatchedTranscriptAcceptancePolicyRowEvidenceIds` and a stable data attribute, keeping malformed owner transcript requests out of the final proof pairing path.

Latest A06 final owner closure payload-shape update: the final owner closure packet now also recomputes the row payload shape by transcript kind. Command rows must carry command text and no route href or section selector, while A18 teaching-route rows must carry href and section selector and no command text. Drift now reports `blocked-owner-transcript-payload-mismatch` with `mismatchedTranscriptPayloadRowEvidenceIds` and a stable data attribute, keeping ambiguous mixed command/route-review rows out of final proof pairing.

Latest A06 final owner closure template evidence-payload update: pending transcript templates are now checked to ensure they do not contain accepted evidence payload fields. Any template that pre-fills `exitCode`, `runId`, `reportPath`, or `reviewDecision` now reports `blocked-owner-transcript-template-evidence-payload-mismatch` with `mismatchedTranscriptTemplateEvidencePayloadRowEvidenceIds` and a stable data attribute. This keeps the owner-gate transcript request layer as a request artifact rather than accepted A11/A18/A22 evidence.

Latest A06 final owner closure proof-ledger count update: the final owner closure packet now recomputes proof-ledger summary counts from proof rows before owner proof pairing. Stale `currentSourceProofReadyCount`, `finalRecordProofCoveredCount`, `pendingFinalRecordProofCount`, `requiredProofEvidenceIdCount`, `requirementCount`, or `remainingOwnerAgentIds` values now report `blocked-final-proof-ledger-count-mismatch` with `proofLedgerCountMismatchReasons` and a stable data attribute, while the more specific missing-proof-owner guard still wins when a proof row is actually absent. This keeps the owner closure layer from displaying stale final proof totals to A11/A18/A22.

Latest A06 closure-evidence update: a new pure TypeScript closure package now joins the objective completion audit to the A11/A18/A22 owner-evidence request packet. It reports `status=owner-closure-required`, `proven=1/4`, `ownerRowCount=3`, `actionRequestCount=11`, `missingEvidenceCount=22`, remaining owners `A11,A18,A22`, and `canMarkThreadGoalComplete=false`. This makes the remaining owner closure rows executable and reviewable; it does not accept evidence or close any A11, A18, or A22 gate.

Latest A06 owner-evidence duplicate-ID hardening update: the completion evidence intake now blocks duplicate submitted owner evidence IDs before the rerun plan can treat A11/A18/A22 evidence as covered. Duplicate owner evidence reports `blocked-invalid-owner-evidence`, exposes `duplicateEvidenceIds`, counts the duplicated submissions as invalid evidence records, sets `readyForGoalGateRerun=false`, and surfaces a stable duplicate-evidence data attribute. This keeps one owner evidence ID from satisfying multiple checklist submissions at the earliest acceptance layer.

Latest A06 owner-evidence request invalid-evidence propagation update: the owner-evidence request packet now carries invalid evidence state from the completion evidence intake instead of returning an ambiguous empty pending packet. When duplicate or otherwise invalid owner evidence is submitted, the packet reports `blocked-invalid-owner-evidence`, exposes `invalidEvidenceRecordCount`, `duplicateEvidenceIds`, and `invalidOwnerAgentIds`, serializes stable invalid-evidence data attributes, and keeps owner rerun planning blocked until A11/A18/A22 evidence is resubmitted cleanly.

Latest A06 completion-rerun update: a pure TypeScript rerun plan now orders the remaining cross-owner closure path. It reports `status=owner-evidence-required`, `stepCount=7`, `ownerEvidenceStepCount=3`, `ownerGateRerunStepCount=3`, `finalAuditStepCount=1`, `missingEvidenceCount=22`, remaining owners `A11,A18,A22`, and `canMarkThreadGoalComplete=false`. The order is owner evidence first, then owner gate reruns, then final objective audit; it does not run or approve A11, A18, or A22 gates. It now also propagates invalid/duplicate owner evidence from the closure package as `blocked-invalid-owner-evidence`, with `invalidEvidenceRecordCount`, `duplicateEvidenceIds`, `invalidOwnerAgentIds`, and stable rerun-plan data attributes before any owner-gate rerun can be trusted.

Latest A06 teaching-source confirmation update: a pure TypeScript source-confirmation ledger now records A06 source readiness for every A18 criterion-level decision without replacing A18 final review. It reports `status=a06-source-confirmed-a18-pending`, `caseCount=12`, `criterionCount=5`, `confirmationCount=60`, `a06ConfirmedDecisionCount=60`, `blockedConfirmationCount=0`, `pendingA18DecisionCount=60`, and `canMarkA18GateComplete=false`.

Latest A06 owner-gate rerun preservation update: the closure package now keeps A11/A18/A22 owner rows after all owner evidence templates are accepted, with `actionRequestCount=0` and `missingEvidenceCount=0` but the three owner gates still incomplete. The rerun plan now converts that state into `status=owner-gate-reruns-required`, `stepCount=4`, `ownerEvidenceStepCount=0`, `ownerGateRerunStepCount=3`, `finalAuditStepCount=1`, and `canMarkThreadGoalComplete=false`, so covered evidence moves owners to gate reruns instead of erasing them from the closure path.

Latest A06 owner-gate rerun intake update: a pure TypeScript intake now validates future A11/A18/A22 owner-gate rerun records from the covered-evidence rerun plan. With no rerun records it reports `status=pending-owner-gate-reruns`, `rerunStepCount=3`, `pendingGateCount=3`, and `canMarkThreadGoalComplete=false`; with all three owner reruns accepted it reports `status=owner-gate-reruns-covered` and `readyForFinalAudit=true` while still refusing final completion until the final objective audit consumes those rerun outputs; with an A22 blocked rerun it reports `status=blocked-owner-gate-rerun`.

Latest A06 final-closure audit update: a pure TypeScript final closure audit now consumes the objective completion audit plus the owner-gate rerun intake. In the current no-owner-records state it reports `status=pending-owner-gate-reruns`, `finalAuditRecordStatus=missing-final-objective-audit`, `ownerGates=0/3`, `proven=1/4`, remaining owners `A11,A18,A22`, and `canMarkThreadGoalComplete=false`. A synthetic all-owner-reruns-plus-final-audit path reaches `status=complete` and `canMarkThreadGoalComplete=true`, but only to prove the closure contract; no real A11, A18, or A22 gate is closed by A06.

Latest A06 owner-gate rerun command packet update: a pure TypeScript command packet now converts the three ready owner-gate rerun steps into executable owner handoff rows. It reports `status=owner-rerun-commands-ready`, owners `A11,A18,A22`, A11 browser commands for HK split and premium reruns, A22 release/preflight/staging commands, `teachingReviewCount=12` A18 rendered-route review rows, and `canMarkThreadGoalComplete=false`. This narrows the remaining owner work from abstract blockers to concrete commands/manual checks without running or approving those gates.

Latest A06 owner-gate rerun command-evidence intake update: a pure TypeScript intake now validates command-row evidence from the owner-gate command packet before creating owner-level gate rerun records. It reports `owner-command-evidence-covered` only when all A11 command rows, all A22 release/preflight/staging rows, and all 12 A18 rendered-route review rows have accepted evidence; missing rows keep the owner pending, blocked rows create blocked owner-gate records, invalid row evidence blocks intake, and `canMarkThreadGoalComplete=false`.

Latest A06 owner-gate rerun command-evidence duplicate-record update: the command-evidence intake now blocks duplicate non-empty command evidence record IDs before owner-level gate rerun records can be synthesized. Duplicate IDs report `blocked-invalid-command-evidence`, expose `duplicateRecordEvidenceIds`, count the duplicated rows as invalid, suppress `ownerGateRerunRecords`, surface a stable duplicate-record data attribute, and keep `canMarkThreadGoalComplete=false` until each A11/A18/A22 command or manual-review row has distinct accepted evidence.

Latest A06 owner-gate rerun command-evidence duplicate-row update: the command-evidence intake now also blocks duplicate `ownerAgentId:rowEvidenceId` evidence submissions before owner-level gate rerun records can be synthesized. Duplicate command evidence rows report `blocked-invalid-command-evidence`, expose `duplicateRecordRowEvidenceIds`, count the duplicated row submissions as invalid, suppress `ownerGateRerunRecords`, surface a stable duplicate-row data attribute, and propagate through the verified closure pipeline before owner gates or final audit can be trusted.

Latest A06 owner-gate rerun command-evidence non-canonical update: the command-evidence intake now rejects row-level command/manual-review evidence IDs that are blank after trimming or would change when trimmed, and exposes padded command evidence IDs as `nonCanonicalRecordEvidenceIds`. A padded command evidence ID now reports `blocked-invalid-command-evidence`, records the submitted row as invalid, serializes `data-viz-manim-v2-owner-gate-rerun-command-evidence-non-canonical-record-ids`, records `nonCanonicalRecordIds=...` in the summary, suppresses owner gate rerun record synthesis, keeps `canMarkThreadGoalComplete=false`, and prevents direct reducer calls from bypassing the canonical transcript-evidence guard.

Latest A06 owner-gate rerun command-evidence missing-id update: the command-evidence intake now exposes row-level command/manual-review records whose submitted `evidenceId` is empty or whitespace-only as `missingRecordEvidenceIdCount`. A blank command evidence ID remains `blocked-invalid-command-evidence`, serializes `data-viz-manim-v2-owner-gate-rerun-command-evidence-missing-record-id-count`, records `missingRecordIds=...` in the summary, suppresses owner gate rerun record synthesis, and gives A11/A18/A22 a distinct missing-ID correction signal instead of only generic invalid command evidence.

Latest A06 owner-gate rerun command-evidence missing-id duplicate-diagnostic update: the command-evidence intake now keeps empty or whitespace-only submitted command/manual-review `evidenceId` values out of `duplicateRecordEvidenceIds`. Two malformed command evidence rows with the same blank evidence ID remain blocked through `missingRecordEvidenceIdCount`, serialize `duplicateRecordIds=none` plus `missingRecordIds=2`, suppress owner gate rerun record synthesis, and keep final closure pending until real canonical command evidence IDs are supplied.

Latest A06 owner-gate rerun command-evidence missing-row-id update: the command-evidence intake now exposes row-level command/manual-review records whose submitted `rowEvidenceId` is empty or whitespace-only as `missingRecordRowEvidenceIdCount`. Latest A06 verified closure pipeline update: a pure TypeScript pipeline now composes command-row evidence intake, owner-gate rerun intake, and final closure audit. Latest A06 command-transcript intake update: a pure TypeScript transcript intake now validates A11/A22 command transcripts and A18 manual-review transcripts before they can become command-evidence records. Passing command transcripts require matching command text and `exitCode=0`; failing command transcripts become blocked evidence; A18 review transcripts require matching href/section selector and `approved`, `revision-required`, or `blocked` decisions; unknown rows or mismatched command text are rejected as invalid.

Unknown rows remain blocked as invalid transcript evidence, serialize `data-viz-manim-v2-command-transcript-unknown-row-ids`, record `unknownTranscriptRows=...` in the summary, suppress command evidence synthesis, and give A11/A18/A22 an exact row correction target before raw transcripts can feed owner-gate records.

Latest A06 owner-gate command-evidence unknown-row update: the command-evidence intake now exposes submitted command/manual-review evidence whose `ownerAgentId:rowEvidenceId` does not match the command packet as `unknownRecordRowEvidenceIds`. Unknown rows remain blocked as invalid command evidence, serialize `data-viz-manim-v2-owner-gate-rerun-command-evidence-unknown-row-ids`, record `unknownRecordRows=...` in the summary, suppress owner-gate rerun record synthesis, and give A11/A18/A22 an exact row correction target before command evidence can feed final closure.

Latest A06 transcript verified-closure pipeline update: a pure TypeScript bridge now starts from raw A11/A22 command transcripts and A18 route-review transcripts, feeds the command-transcript intake, then feeds the verified closure pipeline. It reports `pending-command-transcripts` with no transcripts, `ready-for-final-objective-audit` after all transcripts are accepted and owner gate records are synthesized, and `complete` only when the final objective audit also proves `4/4`; failing command transcripts and invalid transcript rows remain blocking states.

Latest A06 owner-gate transcript request packet update: a pure TypeScript request packet now converts the owner-gate rerun command packet into pending transcript templates for A11, A18, and A22. It reports `pending-owner-transcripts`, owner packets `A11,A18,A22`, command transcript templates for A11/A22 with expected `exitCode=0`, A18 route-review templates with allowed decisions `approved`, `revision-required`, and `blocked`, and `canMarkThreadGoalComplete=false`. Feeding those templates into the transcript intake remains invalid because they deliberately omit exit codes and review decisions; the packet requests evidence but does not manufacture it.

Latest A06 final objective-audit request packet update: a pure TypeScript request packet now asks for the final `4/4` objective-audit record only after A11/A18/A22 owner gate rerun evidence is covered. It reports `blocked-owner-gate-reruns` before owner rows are covered and `pending-final-objective-audit-record` after accepted owner gate rerun evidence IDs exist, carries the exact owner-gate evidence IDs expected by final closure, lists required final-audit fields including `requirementProofEvidenceIds`, preserves current `proven=1/4`, and keeps `canMarkThreadGoalComplete=false`. It is a request/template artifact, not accepted final evidence.

Latest A06 final objective-audit record intake update: a pure TypeScript record intake now validates submitted final objective-audit records against that request packet before final closure can consume them. It reports `pending-final-objective-audit-record` when no record is submitted, `blocked-invalid-final-objective-audit-record` for mismatched owner-gate evidence, wrong proof counts, or missing per-requirement proof evidence IDs, `blocked-final-objective-audit-record` for blocked owner-audit records, and `final-objective-audit-record-accepted` only for an accepted `target=mathSceneV2ObjectiveCompletionAudit` record proving `4/4` with the expected A11/A18/A22 owner-gate evidence IDs plus the four required requirement proof IDs. It deliberately keeps `canMarkThreadGoalComplete=false`; it validates the final evidence shape but does not close the real thread goal.

Latest A06 final objective verified-closure pipeline update: a pure TypeScript pipeline now composes raw owner transcripts, command evidence, final objective-audit request readiness, final objective-audit record intake, and verified closure. It reports `pending-command-transcripts` before A11/A22/A18 transcript evidence exists, `pending-final-objective-audit-record` after transcripts are accepted but no final record is submitted, blocks invalid final records before closure, and reaches `complete` only in the synthetic path where transcripts, command evidence, final record intake, and final closure all accept the same `4/4` objective audit evidence.

Estimated status:

- A06 source/runtime architecture: about 94-95% complete.
- A06 review-package readiness: about 96-97% complete because every Manim file is classified into a review package with anchors and tests, the handoff checklist matrix marks all 8 packages ready with owner/consumer agents, and the new slice planner turns those packages into 20 review batches capped at 24 files.
- A11 focused MAIS Manim browser regression: green for the current function-graph value and mobile-overlap gate.
- A11 broader Visualization Lab regression: red after the post-fix full rerun, but all HK demo-safe grade-split catalog packages from P1 through S6 are now green when run as bounded packages. Split evidence also shows the Manim, configured route, premium route, vector-conic, mobile, and two-lab suspicious subsets are green. A06 now provides a source-backed browser regression package plan plus a typed evidence matrix that chunks HK-demo-safe sweeps, records the observed P1-S6 run IDs/durations, and exposes the live premium variant list including `projection-views`. The remaining broad-sweep evidence points to suite sizing/scoping rather than a direct A06 source crash: unfiltered S4 mixes Mainland PEP labs under an HK demo student, and all-grade HK sweeps can exceed the single-test budget.
- A22 Manim release-slice readiness: about 84-86% complete. Direct root deploy is still blocked by the dirty tree, the owner-approved pruned-staging package and staged Next build path are green, the A06 Manim clean-slice manifest now lists 387 source/test files with `0` forbidden generated/E2E/local-output paths, and the latest targeted A11 browser blockers are resolved, but the broad A11 value-suite red gate and the current disk-space preflight blocker keep full release readiness below the source/runtime completion level.
- A18/A06 teaching-quality confirmation: about 93-94% complete because the automated precheck is green across all 27 registered families, 12 concrete representative cases are A06-confirmed ready for A18 signoff, a grouped A18 human-review brief queues those cases by band with checklist criteria, a typed signoff matrix explicitly tracks all cases as pending A18 final review rather than accepted, a rendered-scene inspection target queue makes the A18 review handoff executable, all 12 cases now have real browser review routes, a review dossier records nine source-backed proof points per case, the final review packet joins the 12 routes to 108 ready proof points with `0` blocked rows, the final decision ledger makes the remaining A18 work `60/60` pending criterion decisions with `canMarkA18GateComplete=false`, the final-decision intake can route A18 revision/block decisions back to A06 follow-up without closing signoff, and the A06 source-confirmation ledger records `60/60` criterion decisions as source-confirmed while keeping A18 acceptance pending. A18 final human/curriculum review is still not closed.
- Overall: about 93-95% engineering-complete. The Manim release slice is about 82-85% release-ready, because the full Visualization Lab browser release gate still needs A11 broad-suite slicing/contract cleanup, A22 disk cleanup / isolated static-asset follow-up, and A18 final signoff.

The A18 final-decision intake now provides the closure path after A18 records criterion-level decisions. The objective completion audit now checks the original four-part objective directly. From current evidence it reports `status=not-complete`, `proven=1/4`, `missingOwnerEvidenceCount=22`, missing owner-evidence groups `A11=6;A22=6;A18+A06=10`, and remaining owners `A11,A18,A22`: A06 review packaging is proven, A11 browser regression still needs owner evidence, A22 clean release is blocked, and A18/A06 teaching quality is still pending final decisions.

The closure evidence package now connects those two closure artifacts. It reports `status=owner-closure-required`, `ownerRowCount=3`, `actionRequestCount=11`, `missingEvidenceCount=22`, and `canMarkThreadGoalComplete=false`, with per-owner rows for A11 browser regression, A18/A06 teaching final decisions, and A22 clean release. When owner evidence is synthetically covered, it keeps the same A11/A18/A22 owner rows with `actionRequestCount=0` and `missingEvidenceCount=0` because the gates still need reruns. It is a closure map for the remaining owners, not a substitute for their acceptance evidence.

The completion rerun plan now orders the remaining closure path after owner evidence is requested. It reports `status=owner-evidence-required`, `stepCount=7`, `ownerEvidenceStepCount=3`, `ownerGateRerunStepCount=3`, `finalAuditStepCount=1`, and `canMarkThreadGoalComplete=false` while evidence is missing. Once owner evidence is covered, it reports `status=owner-gate-reruns-required`, `stepCount=4`, `ownerEvidenceStepCount=0`, and `ownerGateRerunStepCount=3`, keeping final audit blocked until A11/A18/A22 gates are actually rerun. When duplicate or otherwise invalid owner evidence reaches the closure package, the rerun plan reports `blocked-invalid-owner-evidence` and carries invalid evidence counts, duplicate evidence IDs, and invalid owner IDs into the rerun handoff.

The owner-gate rerun intake now validates those future rerun outputs. It reports `pending-owner-gate-reruns` with `3` pending owner gates before records arrive, `owner-gate-reruns-covered` and `readyForFinalAudit=true` when A11/A18/A22 rerun records are all accepted, and `blocked-owner-gate-rerun` when an owner rerun such as A22 clean release remains blocked. It deliberately keeps `canMarkThreadGoalComplete=false` until a final objective audit consumes the rerun intake.

The final closure audit now consumes the objective audit and owner-gate rerun intake. In the current state it reports `pending-owner-gate-reruns`, `finalAuditRecordStatus=missing-final-objective-audit`, `ownerGates=0/3`, `proven=1/4`, and `canMarkThreadGoalComplete=false`; its synthetic complete path only succeeds when all A11/A18/A22 owner rerun evidence IDs match accepted rerun records and the final objective audit proves `4/4`.

The owner-gate rerun command packet now turns the three owner-gate rerun steps into executable handoff rows. It reports `owner-rerun-commands-ready`, owners `A11,A18,A22`, A11 browser rerun commands for the HK split and premium scene packages, A22 release/preflight/staging commands including dry-run cleanup and pruned staging build, 12 A18 rendered-route review rows, and `canMarkThreadGoalComplete=false`.

The owner-gate rerun command-transcript intake now validates actual command/manual-review transcript records before they can become command-evidence records. It requires matching command text and exit codes for A11/A22 command rows, matching href/section selector plus A18 review decisions for rendered-route rows, emits accepted or blocked command evidence records from valid transcripts, keeps missing rows pending, and rejects unknown rows or mismatched commands as invalid.

The owner-gate rerun command-evidence intake now sits between the executable command packet and owner-gate rerun intake. It requires row-level accepted evidence for every A11 browser command, every A22 release/preflight/staging command, and every A18 rendered-route manual review before creating accepted owner gate rerun records; it keeps owners pending on missing rows, creates blocked gate records for blocked rows, rejects unknown row evidence, and keeps `canMarkThreadGoalComplete=false`.

The verified closure pipeline now composes command-row evidence intake, owner-gate rerun intake, and final closure audit into a single completion-facing reducer. It keeps the current state at `pending-command-evidence`, moves to `ready-for-final-objective-audit` only after all A11/A18/A22 command rows are accepted and owner gate records are synthesized, and reaches `complete` only with an accepted final objective audit that proves `4/4`.

The owner-gate transcript request packet now turns the executable A11/A22/A18 owner-gate command rows into pending transcript templates. It records command rows with exact command text and expected `exitCode=0`, A18 rendered-route rows with href/section selector and allowed review decisions, stable required-field lists for each owner, and `canMarkThreadGoalComplete=false`; the templates intentionally omit exit codes and review decisions so they cannot be mistaken for accepted evidence.

The transcript verified-closure pipeline now composes the raw command/manual-review transcript intake directly into the verified closure pipeline. It keeps the current state at `pending-command-transcripts` until raw A11/A22 command transcripts and A18 route-review transcripts arrive, moves to `ready-for-final-objective-audit` only after the transcript intake has produced complete accepted command evidence and owner gate records, blocks invalid/failing transcript rows before final closure, and reaches `complete` only through the same accepted final objective audit path.

The final objective-audit request packet now turns owner-gate coverage into a precise final-audit request. Before A11/A18/A22 command/manual-review rows are accepted it reports `blocked-owner-gate-reruns`; after accepted owner gate rerun evidence exists it reports `pending-final-objective-audit-record`, carries the exact owner-gate evidence IDs expected by `mathSceneV2FinalClosureAudit`, asks for `target=mathSceneV2ObjectiveCompletionAudit`, `status=accepted`, and `provenRequirementCount=4`, and keeps `canMarkThreadGoalComplete=false`.

The final objective-audit record intake now validates the submitted final audit record before final closure consumes it. It requires the request packet to be ready, accepts only `status=accepted`, `target=mathSceneV2ObjectiveCompletionAudit`, `provenRequirementCount=4`, `requirementCount=4`, and the exact expected owner-gate evidence IDs, blocks invalid or blocked records, keeps missing records pending, and keeps `canMarkThreadGoalComplete=false` until the outer final closure audit consumes real owner evidence.

The final objective verified-closure pipeline now composes the last completion path end to end. It starts from raw A11/A22 command transcripts and A18 route-review transcripts, synthesizes command evidence, asks for the final objective audit only after owner rows are covered, validates submitted final audit records through the record intake, and passes only the accepted final audit record into verified closure. The current no-transcript path remains pending; the synthetic all-accepted path proves the reducer can complete without bypassing any final gate.

## Concrete Teaching Review Cases

These 12 scenes are A06-confirmed ready for A18 final signoff. Each case has a learning objective, cognitive-load note, reviewer prompt, semantic bindings, focused timeline beats, and camera-shot evidence.

The A18 review brief now groups the same 12 cases into a human signoff queue by band: primary `2`, middle-school `1`, secondary `5`, and advanced `4`. The required checklist is `curriculum-fit`, `mathematical-accuracy`, `cognitive-load`, `language-and-labels`, and `interaction-timing`; this is a stronger handoff artifact, not a replacement for A18 final review.

The A18 signoff matrix now mirrors all 12 concrete cases, keeps the gate at `a18-final-signoff-required`, marks every case `pending-a18-review`, and exposes required A18 actions: inspect rendered scene, confirm curriculum fit, confirm mathematical accuracy, confirm cognitive load, confirm language/labels, confirm interaction timing, and record approve-or-revision decision.

The A18 rendered-scene inspection target queue now mirrors the same 12 signoff rows, preserves each concrete `familyId` and `sceneId`, exposes the rendered selectors `data-viz-family-id`, `data-viz-scene-id`, `data-viz-manim-scene-selector-selected-family-id`, and `data-viz-manim-scene-selector-selected-scene-id`, and links those selectors to the source-evidence attributes for teaching quality, review cases, review brief, and signoff status. All 12 targets are `ready-for-a18-rendered-scene-review` and remain `pending-a18-review`.

The A18 teaching review dossier now joins those targets back to actual scene-spec teaching-quality evidence. Each case has nine ready proof points: runtime export approval, visible math objects, formula tokens, semantic formula bindings, focused timeline beats, camera shots, controlled parameters, rendered selector contract, and diagnostic counts. The dossier keeps all human decisions `pending-a18-review`; it strengthens the handoff but does not replace A18 final judgment.

The A18 final rendered teaching review packet now joins the 12 browser review routes to the dossier evidence. It records `caseCount=12`, `renderedRouteReadyCount=12`, `readyProofPointCount=108`, `totalProofPointCount=108`, `blockedRowCount=0`, and `pendingA18DecisionCount=12`, so A18 can inspect rendered scenes with the matching source proof points without the system pretending those scenes are already accepted.

- `number-line-core`: `mais-manim-number-line`, primary, 4 bindings, 7 focused beats, 2 camera shots. Objective: value point, unit interval, and comparison distance on a number line.
- `fraction-slices-core`: `mais-manim-fraction-slices`, primary, 4 bindings, 7 focused beats, 2 camera shots. Objective: whole, selected part, denominator, and unit fraction.
- `angle-geometry-core`: `mais-manim-angle-geometry`, middle-school, 4 bindings, 7 focused beats, 2 camera shots. Objective: angle measure as rotation from reference ray to moving ray.
- `function-graph-core`: `mais-manim-function-graph`, secondary, 2 bindings, 6 focused beats, 2 camera shots. Objective: function rule producing a graph point as input changes.
- `function-family-core`: `mais-manim-function-family`, secondary, 3 bindings, 6 focused beats, 2 camera shots. Objective: parameter changes transforming a function family.
- `trig-unit-wave-core`: `mais-manim-trig-unit-wave`, secondary, 4 bindings, 7 focused beats, 3 camera shots. Objective: unit-circle motion connected to sine/cosine wave.
- `calculus-rate-area-core`: `mais-manim-calculus-rate-area`, advanced, 4 bindings, 7 focused beats, 3 camera shots. Objective: local rate of change versus accumulated area.
- `conic-section-core`: `mais-manim-conic-sections-deep`, advanced, 4 bindings, 8 focused beats, 3 camera shots. Objective: plane slice through a cone generating a conic curve.
- `space-vectors-core`: `mais-manim-space-vectors-lines-planes`, advanced, 4 bindings, 8 focused beats, 3 camera shots. Objective: vectors, lines, and planes encoding 3D direction and position.
- `probability-machine-core`: `mais-manim-probability-machine`, secondary, 4 bindings, 8 focused beats, 3 camera shots. Objective: repeated trial paths connected to empirical probability.
- `statistics-distribution-core`: `mais-manim-statistics-distribution`, secondary, 4 bindings, 8 focused beats, 3 camera shots. Objective: center and spread of a distribution.
- `statistical-inference-core`: `mais-manim-statistical-inference-lab`, advanced, 4 bindings, 8 focused beats, 3 camera shots. Objective: sample-based interval estimate with visible uncertainty.
